# MergePilot Business Test Execution Report

## Purpose

This report records business-level test execution against the running MergePilot app. It complements `docs/agent-business-test-checklist.md` and `docs/automated-business-test-suite-plan.md`.

The focus is product behavior, not only code quality:

- Can the running app receive and complete real Chat requests?
- Does the agent respect read-only user intent?
- Does it use Project Link, Git, Azure DevOps, and pipeline context safely?
- Are failures actionable and traceable?

## Current Runtime

Latest installed-daemon reprobe collected on 2026-07-17. Frontend browser gates start their own Vite server when needed and are recorded separately below.

| Area | Value |
|---|---|
| Frontend | Started by Playwright/Vite for mocked browser gates; not left running after the latest reprobe |
| Daemon | `http://127.0.0.1:8787` |
| Runtime version | `0.5.20` |
| Model provider | Azure OpenAI |
| Chat deployment | `gpt-4o` |
| Config source | `C:\Users\15492\.mergepilot\config.toml` |
| Cloud Project Link store | Enabled, with local fallback |
| Cloud secrets | Disabled |
| Cloud sessions | Enabled |


|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GIT-W-01 / CHAT-05 | Stage all changes requires approval | Clicked `Stage and commit` for temp repo with untracked `feature.txt`, then approved `git add -A`. | Pass | Temp repo moved from `?? feature.txt` to staged `A  feature.txt`. | Staging remains correctly approval-gated and scoped to the temp repo. |
| GIT-W-03 / CHAT-05 | Commit staged changes after approval | Approved the follow-up `git commit -m "chore: add feature"` card. | Fail | The UI continued to show the same commit approval/executing state; repo commit count stayed `1`, last commit stayed `Initial commit`, and `feature.txt` remained staged. | Commit approval is not reliably completing after a successful stage action. This blocks the normal `Stage and commit` business workflow even though the stage sub-step works. |
| CHAT-04 / UI-06 | Transcript/history after approval | Observed Chat history and transcript after the failed commit attempt. | Partial | History still contained raw `[confirmed & executed] git_add({"all":true})` JSON from previous approved actions. | Session title sanitization remains open and should be covered by a regression test. |
| DATA / Cleanup | Clean up test state | Deleted temp Project Link `cac1ea442607b706` and temp directory `mp-git-commit-20260705-030717`. | Pass | `projectLinkDeleted: true`, `tempRootRemoved: true`. | No temporary Project Link or repo was intentionally retained. |

### Stage And Commit Fix Retest

Root cause: after the stage action completed, the daemon correctly emitted a follow-up commit approval, but the desktop stream dispatcher kept the UI in `busy` state. The visible commit approval card could be clicked, but `confirmPendingAction` ignored the click because `busy === true`. The fix releases busy/cancel state when `workflow_state.pendingApproval` or `approval_required` arrives.

Retest run:

```text
output/playwright/git-write-smoke-20260705/mp-git-commit-fixed-20260705-031456.png
```

| Checklist ID | Scenario | Action | Result | Evidence | Finding |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CHAT-05 / GIT-W-01 | Approve stage step | Clicked `Stage and commit`, then approved `git add -A`. | Pass | Temp repo staged `feature.txt` after 2 polling intervals. | The first approval still works after the dispatcher fix. |
| CHAT-05 / GIT-W-03 / GIT-W-05 | Approve follow-up commit step | Approved `git commit -m "chore: add feature"` after the stage approval. | Pass | Commit count changed from `1` to `2`; last commit was `f13521d chore: add feature`; final `git status --short` was empty. | Fixed: follow-up approval is clickable and reaches the daemon after stage completion. |
| DATA / Cleanup | Clean up retest state | Deleted temp Project Link `65b1e56167f6e26e` and temp directory `mp-git-commit-fixed-20260705-031456`. | Pass | `projectLinkDeleted: true`, `tempRootRemoved: true`. | No temporary Project Link or repo was retained. |

## Next Test Targets

| Target | Why |
|---|---|
| Hosted pipeline capacity or self-hosted pool proof | Destructive ADO trigger now queues the correct ClaimBot_API pipeline and cleans up the run. The remaining success-status proof is blocked by Azure DevOps hosted minutes being exhausted. |
| Windows release signing | Local `0.5.22` MSI/NSIS artifacts and current published Windows assets are unsigned. This remains the direct cause of Windows unknown-publisher prompts until trusted Authenticode signing is wired into release and the required signing secrets/certificate are configured. |
| Heavy renderer code splitting | Chat source preview, artifact content, and non-chat route pages now lazy-load successfully; the main app entry is no longer the source of Vite's large-chunk warning. Large optional chunks remain for Mermaid, Wardley, Cytoscape, KaTeX, and CodeMirror vendor code. |
| Version-bumped installed MSI parity | Current worktree `0.5.22` MSI and NSIS artifacts have been rebuilt and pass non-installing payload smoke. The install verifier now surfaces nested verify/vision summaries, cleans up failure-path probe daemons, and refuses unrelated port owners. The live-app wrapper also refuses unrelated `8787` owners before replacing mismatched daemons. The remaining proof is to install the rebuilt `MergePilot_0.5.22_x64_en-US.msi` from an elevated shell and rerun installed-app state/native smoke against Program Files. |
| Live Review Queue cloud persistence after Azure Table permission | Local/mocked Review Queue and live fallback behavior pass, including PR `#2655` visible from `/review-queue` when Azure Table is unavailable. True Azure Table `ReviewHistory` persistence still needs Storage Table data-plane permission before it can be proven end-to-end. |
| Native installed UI pixel/click smoke | Computer Use import/runtime is repaired locally and can enumerate/read the installed MergePilot window after patching the plugin cache to avoid the `@oai/sky` package-exports deep import. Latest 2026-07-06 09:40-09:47 native recheck confirmed bootstrap, app/window discovery, and accessibility text for `New chat`, `Project Links`, `Review Queue`, `Pipelines`, `Settings`, signed-in user `Zhou Ping`, `ClaimBot_API link`, and `TeBS-ClaimBot / ClaimBot_API`; release-grade native UI smoke still needs reliable screenshot/activation/click proof because screenshots for both app-id and process-backed handles show unrelated river imagery, `activate_window` fails, and a low-risk Settings click fails with `failed to activate captured window`. The plugin-cache repair is local and may be overwritten by Codex/plugin updates. Record: `docs/live-e2e-test-records.md#run-mp-installed-computer-use-recheck-20260706-0940`. |
| Live ADO-backed Review Queue cloud records | Needed after Storage Table data-plane permission is granted. Current runtime fallback record is live and useful, but not proof that the `ReviewHistory` entity is written/read from Azure Table Storage. |
| Installed app first-run UI smoke | Current installed `0.5.20` state/auth/avatar/persistence/live-vision gates pass. The remaining strict proof is for a fresh elevated install of rebuilt `0.5.22`, followed by installed-app state/native UI smoke against Program Files. |
| Installed live vision stream parity | Source daemon, rebuilt packaged sidecar, earlier MSI-extracted daemons, published `v0.5.20` MSI-extracted daemon, and the currently installed Program Files `0.5.20` daemon now prove live vision with strict retry. The stricter harness caught one transient installed `0.5.20` `read ECONNRESET`, then a clean retry passed. Local packaged `0.5.22` also passed strict SSE cleanliness. The remaining parity gap is for rebuilt local `0.5.22`, which has not been installed system-wide. |
